## Qué no pude verificar

Nada quedó sin verificar dentro del alcance (`catalog.py` + `tests/test_routing.py`): los dos casos del
hallazgo, las cuatro (en realidad seis) formas adicionales de romper el archivo, el caso legítimo de
no-regresión, la mordida en las dos direcciones y los cuatro gates corrieron todos, con salida literal
pegada arriba.

Un dato que quedó fuera de mi alcance explícito y que el propio enunciado reserva para el orquestador:
la captura A/B del efecto colateral a nivel de todo el paquete (más allá de la prueba puntual de
no-regresión que sí corrí acá, aislada a `pi_auth_provider_keys`/`_pi_auth_signature`/`_probe_pairs`).

--- catalog context ---
   300	    the audited set past what is actually usable, the opposite of fail-closed. Live-measured
   301	    2026-08-10 (opencode 1.18.14): every row on this machine is `●`, so this fix has no
   302	    observable effect on today's real inventory -- it closes the defect regardless."""
   303	    providers = set()
   304	    for line in _clean_lines(stdout):
   305	        if line.startswith("Error"):
   306	            raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   307	        if line.startswith(("●", "*")):
   308	            body = line.lstrip("●* ").strip()
   309	            if body:
   310	                # The trailing word is the credential method (api/oauth); the rest is the provider name.
   311	                name = body.rsplit(None, 1)[0] if len(body.split()) > 1 else body
   312	                providers.add(name.strip().lower())
   313	    return providers
   314	
   315	
   316	def _parse_opencode_models(stdout: str, provider_key: str) -> set[str]:
   317	    """`opencode models <provider> --pure` prints `<provider>/<model>` lines; errors exit 0, so text is authoritative."""
   318	    models = set()
   319	    for line in _clean_lines(stdout):
   320	        if line.startswith("Error"):
   321	            raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   322	        prefix = provider_key + "/"
   323	        if not line.startswith(prefix):
   324	            raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   325	        model = line[len(prefix):].strip()
   326	        if model:
   327	            models.add(model)
   328	    return models
   329	
   330	
   331	def _parse_pi_models(stdout: str, provider: str) -> set[str]:
   332	    """`pi --list-models` prints a `provider  model  ...` column table (header row first,
   333	    columns whitespace-separated); only rows whose first column matches `provider` are
   334	    read. openai-codex ids are catalog-IDENTITY (returned verbatim, spike-verified);
   335	    anthropic raw ids are translated through PI_MODEL_MAP so callers intersect against the
   336	    SAME short-name vocabulary every other pair uses — a raw Pi id never leaks through
   337	    untranslated. An unrecognized header shape fails closed (PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED),
   338	    same discipline as the opencode parsers above."""
   339	    lines = _clean_lines(stdout)
   340	    if not lines or lines[0].split()[:2] != ["provider", "model"]:
   341	        raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   342	    raw = {parts[1] for line in lines[1:] if len(parts := line.split()) >= 2 and parts[0] == provider}
   343	    if provider == "openai-codex":
   344	        return raw
   345	    return {short for short, canonical in PI_MODEL_MAP.get(provider, {}).items() if canonical in raw}
   346	
   347	
   348	def pi_auth_provider_keys() -> frozenset[str]:
   349	    """Read-only key-SET of `~/.pi/agent/auth.json` (spike Q2): provider NAMES only, never
   350	    token values, never logged. Any surprise (missing file, symlink, foreign-owned file,
   351	    foreign shape, bad JSON) is an empty set, fail-closed — this never raises and the
   352	    caller never sees a credential value, only whether a provider name is present as a
   353	    key. P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3): the uid check was missing here even though
   354	    `_read_credential_json` (below) has always had it -- a file owned by another uid on
   355	    the same box could be read and its keys folded into a routing decision (the real
   356	    gate at `_probe_pairs`, `provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys()`) as if it were this
   357	    user's own credential state. Same discipline as `_read_credential_json` now: `lstat`,
   358	    never a symlink, a regular file, owned by THIS uid.
   359	
   360	    P3-F03 repair (022 PKG-3 repair round 2): a top-level key is kept ONLY when it is
   361	    both (a) a provider `_PAIR_COMMANDS` actually audits for the `pi` runtime
   362	    (`openai-codex`/`anthropic` today -- exactly the two providers the sole call site,
   363	    `_probe_pairs`'s `provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys()` gate at catalog.py:803,
   364	    can ever ask about) and (b) mapped to a JSON OBJECT, never any other value shape.
   365	    Before this fix the docstring above already promised "provider NAMES only" but the
   366	    implementation kept every top-level string key verbatim -- an unaudited key
   367	    (`{"proveedor-inventado": {...}}`) or an audited key holding a non-object value
   368	    (`{"openai-codex": []}`) both survived into the returned set, silently widening the
   369	    routing gate past what this file actually audits. Filtering to the SAME set
   370	    `resolve_discovered_providers` already treats as the pi-runtime audited universe
   371	    means a legitimate `{"openai-codex": {...}, "anthropic": {...}}` file is completely
   372	    unaffected -- see test_pi_auth_provider_keys_rejects_unaudited_and_non_object_entries."""
   373	    path = Path.home() / ".pi/agent/auth.json"
   374	    try:
   375	        st = path.lstat()
   376	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   377	            return frozenset()
   378	        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   379	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   380	        return frozenset()
   381	    if not isinstance(doc, dict):
   382	        return frozenset()
   383	    audited = {provider for runtime, provider in _PAIR_COMMANDS if runtime == "pi"}
   384	    return frozenset(key for key, value in doc.items()
   385	                      if isinstance(key, str) and key in audited and isinstance(value, dict))
   386	
   387	
   388	def _binary_signature(name: str) -> str:
   389	    """ADR-0034 (AC-08), generalized by ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-07): path + mtime of
   390	    `name`'s resolved binary on PATH right now -- swapping the binary (upgrade,
   391	    reinstall, a different shim) invalidates every existing cache entry without a
   392	    manual version bump. Originally scoped to `opencode` alone; AC-07 extends the same
   393	    argument to `codex` and `claude` too -- any of the three CLI binaries can change how
   394	    it reads/writes its OWN credential store on a swap, so each binary's signature is
   395	    folded into `_cache_key` alongside its runtime's credential-file signature (below).
   396	    No subprocess here, just a PATH lookup and a stat -- safe to call from a pure
   397	    hashing function. Unresolvable is a fixed per-name sentinel that simply never
   398	    equals a real signature, so the cache is skipped (fail-closed), never fabricated as
   399	    a match."""
   400	    path = shutil.which(name)
   401	    if not path:
   402	        return f"{name}:absent"
   403	    try:
   404	        mtime_ns = os.stat(path).st_mtime_ns
   405	    except OSError:
   406	        return f"{name}:absent"
   407	    return f"{name}:{path}:{mtime_ns}"
   408	
   409	
   410	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3)
   411	# Per-runtime credential signatures, AC-07: closes "de-auth invisible for up to
   412	# PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) in 3 of 4 runtimes" -- `_cache_key` used to fold in ONLY
   413	# opencode's live subprocess-based auth signature (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`
   414	# above). Every signature below is `stat`/local-read only -- ZERO new subprocesses
   415	# (the one subprocess this whole cache-key computation ever pays stays
   416	# `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the CALLER and passed in, never invoked
   417	# from here -- see `test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_
   418	# signatures` in tests/test_routing.py and evidence/P3-implementer.md's proof). Same
   419	# security discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` throughout (AC-09): `lstat` (never a
   420	# symlink), a regular file, this uid, ONLY named fields ever read -- never a whole-file
   421	# hash or mtime, because `~/.claude/.credentials.json` also holds an unrelated
   422	# `mcpOAuth` block (today a Vercel token, live-measured) that rotates on ITS OWN MCP
   423	# refresh cycle with zero relation to the Claude Code provider credential; hashing the
   424	# file or its mtime would rotate this signature on every MCP token refresh, defeating
   425	# the cache for a reason that has nothing to do with what this signature exists to
   426	# track. Every value is hashed before being folded into `_cache_key` -- never logged,
   427	# never placed in an envelope, never a raw credential value.
   428	
   429	
   430	def _read_credential_json(path: Path) -> dict | None:
   431	    """Shared fail-closed read for a local per-runtime credential file: `lstat` (never
   432	    a symlink), a regular file, owned by this uid, valid JSON, a dict -- ANY surprise is
   433	    `None`, never raised, same discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` above. The caller
   434	    reads only specific NAMED fields from the result; this function itself never
   435	    inspects field values, so widening what a signature reads is always a one-line,
   436	    auditable change at the call site, never here."""
   437	    try:
   438	        st = path.lstat()
   439	        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   440	            return None

# never appears in `_PAIR_COMMANDS`, so `_probe_pairs` never even queries it -- the rest
# of the inventory is never at risk of an unverified id leaking in. `--route-doctor`
# (PKG-2, ADR-0035) is the surface that will report "detected, unlistable" to a human;
# nothing here needs to change for that report to be accurate.
# The audited pair table is the single source of runtime/provider compatibility:
# a pair absent here can never authenticate, list models, or appear in identities.
# 012 AC-01: two OpenCode-lane pairs added, `opencode-zen`/`opencode-go` — real,
# currently-authenticated OpenCode providers that were simply outside this table

# Repair F-05 (012 repair): the opencode-lane argv is DERIVED from _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS
# above, never a second hardcoded copy of the CLI id — a future change to one map with

# test or the live gate (both only ever exercise whichever id `_PAIR_COMMANDS` itself

_PAIR_COMMANDS = {

    ("claude-code", "anthropic"): (("claude", "auth", "status", "--json"),),
    **{("opencode", provider): (("opencode", "auth", "list", "--pure"), ("opencode", "models", cli_id, "--pure"))
       for provider, cli_id in _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS.items()},

   390	    `name`'s resolved binary on PATH right now -- swapping the binary (upgrade,

   521	def _pi_auth_signature() -> str:
   522	    """AC-07: pi's per-runtime signature folds `pi_auth_provider_keys()` (already
   523	    stat/local-read only, zero subprocess, spike Q2 discipline -- untouched by this
   524	    package beyond the uid check, P3-SEC-002) with `PI_PINNED_VERSION` (the EXACT pi
   525	    release the probe invokes, above) -- a version bump is itself credential-relevant: a
   526	    different pinned pi release could read/write a differently-shaped auth.json. Provider
   527	    NAMES only (never a token value), hashed before folding into `_cache_key`.
   528	
   529	    P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): an EMPTY key-set (file absent, symlink,
   530	    foreign-owned, corrupt/foreign-shaped JSON -- anything `pi_auth_provider_keys()`
   531	    itself already fails closed on) must fail closed HERE too, to the empty string, same
   532	    as `_codex_auth_signature`/`_claude_code_auth_signature` above. Before this fix, an
   533	    empty key-set was folded into a hash alongside `PI_PINNED_VERSION` regardless,
   534	    producing a fixed non-empty signature for "pi has no credentials" -- fail-OPEN,
   535	    because a non-empty signature is indistinguishable, at the `_cache_key` level, from a
   536	    real credential state. This is safe to change: `_pi_auth_signature`'s only consumer
   537	    is `_cache_key` (cache freshness), never the real per-pair authentication gate
   538	    (`_probe_pairs` calls `pi_auth_provider_keys()` directly, catalog.py:753) -- an empty
   539	    signature can only ever cost a cache miss (a fresh, correctly-gated probe runs next),
   540	    never fabricate or suppress which pairs are actually authenticated."""
   541	    keys = pi_auth_provider_keys()
   542	    if not keys:
   543	        return ""
   544	    names = ",".join(sorted(keys))
   545	    return hashlib.sha256(f"{names}|{PI_PINNED_VERSION}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

   735	def _probe_pairs(config: dict, pairs, timeout: float, ran=None) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
   736	    """Fresh, uncached, pair-scoped subprocess observation for exactly `pairs`.
   737	
   738	    Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
   739	    error text — makes only that pair unavailable (never raises). A pair
   740	    without any resulting model is simply absent from the returned dict:
   741	    negative results are never a first-class cached value (F06).
   742	    """
   743	    result: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
   744	    # Identical argvs across pairs (e.g. `opencode auth list` for both opencode pairs) run once.
   745	    ran = {} if ran is None else ran
   746	    # Non-interactive probe environment: opencode's TUI writer blocks forever on a
   747	    # non-TTY stdout unless CI/TERM signal a dumb terminal (observed 2026-07-26).
   748	    probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
   749	    for pair in pairs:
   750	        runtime, provider = pair
   751	        commands = _PAIR_COMMANDS[pair]
   752	        # ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2, AC-04): "veto" is dropped before any subprocess runs at
   753	        # all -- same early-exit position the pre-tri-state `if not allowed: continue`
   754	        # had, so a curated-empty provider still costs nothing to probe. "auto" (no
   755	        # `[catalog]` key for this provider) used to fall into that SAME branch (an empty
   756	        # `set()` is falsy too) and get skipped just the same -- exactly the defect this
   757	        # package removes: `allowed` being `None` below is deliberately NOT treated as
   758	        # falsy-skip; only genuine credential/parse failure further down drops the pair.
   759	        state, allowed = resolve_ceiling(config, provider)
   760	        if state == "veto":
   761	            continue
   762	        # PKG-N02: only the pi pairs get the cold-pnpm floor; every other pair keeps the
   763	        # caller's own timeout unchanged.
   764	        pair_timeout = max(timeout, PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) if runtime == "pi" else timeout
   765	        if runtime == "opencode":
   766	            # 012 AC-02: the credential-set check (map 1) and the models-listing CLI
   767	            # id/prefix (map 2) are two independently addressable lookups, never one.
   768	            # Repair F-06 (012 repair, medium, +69%/+10.5s measured without this fix):
   769	            # map 1's membership check runs BEFORE the second, more expensive `opencode
   770	            # models <id>` call, not after both commands already ran — a machine missing
   771	            # a given OpenCode-lane subscription now never pays that pair's models-call
   772	            # latency at all, instead of always paying it only to discard the result.
   773	            auth_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[0], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   774	            if auth_result is None or auth_result.returncode != 0:
   775	                continue
   776	            try:
   777	                provider_key = _OPENCODE_PROVIDER_KEYS[provider]
   778	                credentials = _parse_opencode_auth(auth_result.stdout)
   779	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   780	                continue
   781	            if provider_key not in credentials:
   782	                continue
   783	            models_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[1], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   784	            if models_result is None or models_result.returncode != 0:
   785	                continue
   786	            try:
   787	                cli_id = _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS[provider]
   788	                parsed = _parse_opencode_models(models_result.stdout, cli_id)
   789	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   790	                continue
   791	            # AC-05 layer 3 is unaffected by "auto": billing_rank's fail-closed-to-
   792	            # expensive default already applies to any provider PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND
   793	            # does not classify as "subscription" -- ceiling state never enters that map.
   794	            models = parsed if state == "auto" else (allowed & parsed)
   795	        else:
   796	            completed, failed = [], False
   797	            for argv in commands:
   798	                completed_item = _run_cached(ran, argv, pair_timeout, probe_env)
   799	                if completed_item is None:
   800	                    failed = True; break
   801	                completed.append(completed_item)
   802	            if failed or any(item.returncode != 0 for item in completed):
   803	                continue
   804	            try:
   805	                if runtime == "codex":
   806	                    # ADR-0042 AC-04: codex/claude-code never see state == "auto" here --
   807	                    # `models_config.load_config` dies if [catalog].codex/.claude are
   808	                    # absent or empty, so `allowed` is always the curated set by the time
   809	                    # a validated config reaches this function (see resolve_ceiling's
   810	                    # own docstring). Neither runtime has a models-LISTING command to
   811	                    # fall back on if it ever were "auto" (only a login-status boolean).
   812	                    models = allowed if _parse_codex_login(completed[0].stdout, completed[0].stderr) else set()
   813	                elif runtime == "claude-code":
   814	                    models = allowed if _parse_claude_auth(completed[0].stdout) else set()

   816	                    # Belt-and-suspenders (T-305, spike Q2): the auth.json key-set is a cheap,
   817	                    # non-subprocess signal alongside the naturally fail-closed column-parse
   818	                    # below (an unauthenticated provider simply has no rows in --list-models).
   819	                    if provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys():
   820	                        continue
   821	                    # Same guarantee as codex/claude-code above: pi's two pairs are both

   824	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   825	                continue
   826	        if models:
   827	            result[pair] = models
   828	    return result

  2654	            (features / "bad.json").write_text(json.dumps({"feature_id": "bad", "packages": {"not": "a list"}}))
  2655	            (features / "also-bad.json").write_text(json.dumps({"feature_id": "also-bad", "packages": ["not-a-dict"]}))
  2656	            old_root, old_project = set_agents_app.ROOT, set_agents_app.PROJECT_ROOT
  2657	            set_agents_app.ROOT = root
  2658	            set_agents_app.PROJECT_ROOT = root
  2659	            try:
  2660	                self.assertIsNone(set_agents_app._load_feature_doc(features / "bad.json"))
  2661	                self.assertEqual(set_agents_app._resolve_context_pack("bad", None), (False, "bad", None))
  2662	                self.assertEqual(set_agents_app._resolve_context_pack(None, None), (None, None, None))
  2663	            finally:
  2664	                set_agents_app.ROOT, set_agents_app.PROJECT_ROOT = old_root, old_project

  2666	    def test_schema_four_warning_is_informational_and_never_auto_migrates(self):

  2697	    def test_route_decide_cli_hermetic_matrix(self):
  2698	        # N04/F01/F02/F07: a fully hermetic decide->dispatched->terminal(->abandoned) cycle
  2699	        # against a temp routing root (the F07 seam), driving every role_class shape of the
  2700	        # F01 reason->exit table through the real CLI subprocess.
  2701	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2702	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  2703	            env=self._cli_env(Path(td)/"routing-root", bins)
  2704	
  2705	            def decide(descriptor):
  2706	                return self._cli_run(["--route-decide","-","--json"],env,json.dumps(descriptor))
  2707	
  2708	            # Writer: executable, ok=true, exit 0.
  2709	            writer=decide({"role":"implementer","task_class":"mechanical","selected_runtime":"codex"})
  2710	            self.assertEqual(writer.returncode,0,(writer.stdout,writer.stderr))
  2711	            wdata=json.loads(writer.stdout)
  2712	            self.assertTrue(wdata["ok"]); self.assertTrue(wdata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2713	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present.
  2714	            self.assertEqual(len(wdata["data"]["reason_codes"]),1)
  2715	            self.assertTrue(wdata["data"]["reason_codes"][0].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2716	            run_id=wdata["data"]["run_id"]; self.assertTrue(run_id.startswith("run1_"))
  2717	            self.assertEqual(wdata["data"]["tier"],"fast")
  2718	
  2719	            # docs-rw ("other" role_class): non-executable, empty reason_codes, still ok=true.
  2720	            other=decide({"role":"product-analyst","task_class":"documentation","selected_runtime":"claude-code"})
  2721	            self.assertEqual(other.returncode,0,(other.stdout,other.stderr))
  2722	            odata=json.loads(other.stdout)
  2723	            self.assertTrue(odata["ok"]); self.assertFalse(odata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2724	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present.
  2725	            self.assertEqual(len(odata["data"]["reason_codes"]),1)
  2726	            self.assertTrue(odata["data"]["reason_codes"][0].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2727	
  2728	            # Reviewer without review_of_run_id: unverified, ok=true, exit 0, tier still reported.
  2729	            unverified=decide({"role":"package-reviewer","task_class":"documentation","selected_runtime":"claude-code"})
  2730	            self.assertEqual(unverified.returncode,0,(unverified.stdout,unverified.stderr))
  2731	            udata=json.loads(unverified.stdout)
  2732	            self.assertTrue(udata["ok"]); self.assertFalse(udata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2733	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present alongside
  2734	            # REVIEW_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED.
  2735	            self.assertEqual(udata["data"]["reason_codes"][0],"REVIEW_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED")
  2736	            self.assertTrue(udata["data"]["reason_codes"][1].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2737	            self.assertEqual(len(udata["data"]["reason_codes"]),2)
  2738	            self.assertIsNotNone(udata["data"]["model"])
  2739	            self.assertFalse(udata["data"]["independence_verified"])
  2740	
  2741	            # An unknown role: ok=false, exit 1 — the "other side" of the F01 table.
  2742	            unknown=decide({"role":"nonexistent-role","task_class":"documentation"})
  2743	            self.assertEqual(unknown.returncode,1,(unknown.stdout,unknown.stderr))
  2744	            self.assertFalse(json.loads(unknown.stdout)["ok"])
  2745	
  2746	            # F07/lifecycle: dispatched -> terminal(success), all via CLI against the temp root.
  2747	            dispatched=self._cli_run(["--route-dispatched",run_id,"--json"],env)
  2748	            self.assertEqual(dispatched.returncode,0,(dispatched.stdout,dispatched.stderr))
  2749	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(dispatched.stdout)["data"]["state"],"dispatched")
  2750	            terminal=self._cli_run(["--route-terminal",run_id,"success","--json"],env)
  2751	            self.assertEqual(terminal.returncode,0,(terminal.stdout,terminal.stderr))
  2752	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(terminal.stdout)["data"]["state"],"terminal_success")
  2753	
  2754	            recent=self._cli_run(["--routing-recent-writers","--json"],env)
  2755	            self.assertEqual(recent.returncode,0)
  2756	            self.assertEqual([r["run_id"] for r in json.loads(recent.stdout)["data"]["recent_writers"]],[run_id])
  2757	
  2758	            # Reviewer WITH a verified review_of_run_id: a candidate is found (a different
  2759	            # provider/family than the writer above), ok=true, independence_verified true.
  2760	            verified=decide({"role":"package-reviewer","task_class":"documentation","selected_runtime":"claude-code",
  2761	                             "review_of_run_id":run_id})
  2762	            self.assertEqual(verified.returncode,0,(verified.stdout,verified.stderr))
  2763	            vdata=json.loads(verified.stdout)
  2764	            self.assertTrue(vdata["ok"]); self.assertFalse(vdata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2765	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present.
  2766	            self.assertEqual(len(vdata["data"]["reason_codes"]),1)
  2767	            self.assertTrue(vdata["data"]["reason_codes"][0].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2768	            self.assertTrue(vdata["data"]["independence_verified"])
  2769	            self.assertNotEqual(vdata["data"]["provider"],"openai-codex")
  2770	
  2771	            # A second writer decide, closed as failure BEFORE dispatch -> abandoned (F02/F07).
  2772	            second=decide({"role":"implementer","task_class":"mechanical","selected_runtime":"codex"})
  2773	            second_run=json.loads(second.stdout)["data"]["run_id"]
  2774	            abandoned=self._cli_run(["--route-terminal",second_run,"failure","--json"],env)
  2775	            self.assertEqual(abandoned.returncode,0,(abandoned.stdout,abandoned.stderr))
  2776	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(abandoned.stdout)["data"]["state"],"abandoned")
  2777	            open_runs=json.loads(self._cli_run(["--routing-open-runs","--json"],env).stdout)["data"]["open_runs"]
  2778	            self.assertEqual(open_runs,[])
  2779	
  2780	    def test_route_decide_cli_lock_fails_closed(self):
  2781	        # F01: SQLITE busy (holder BEGIN IMMEDIATE) ⇒ ROUTING_UNAVAILABLE, exit 1, no retry.
  2782	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2783	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  2784	            routing_root=Path(td)/"routing-root"
  2785	            env=self._cli_env(routing_root, bins)
  2786	            descriptor=json.dumps({"role":"implementer","task_class":"mechanical","selected_runtime":"codex"})
  2787	            first=self._cli_run(["--route-decide","-","--json"],env,descriptor)
  2788	            self.assertEqual(first.returncode,0,(first.stdout,first.stderr))
  2789	            holder=sqlite3.connect(routing_root/"routing.db")
  2790	            holder.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=0")
  2791	            holder.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  2792	            try:
  2793	                locked=self._cli_run(["--route-decide","-","--json"],env,descriptor)
  2794	                self.assertEqual(locked.returncode,1,(locked.stdout,locked.stderr))
  2795	                ldata=json.loads(locked.stdout)
  2796	                self.assertFalse(ldata["ok"]); self.assertEqual(ldata["reason_codes"],["ROUTING_UNAVAILABLE"])
  2797	            finally:
  2798	                holder.execute("ROLLBACK"); holder.close()

  2800	    def test_route_terminal_latency_bound_rejects_without_traceback(self):
  2801	        # SEC-A02: an out-of-range (OverflowError-at-bind-time) or negative --latency-ms is a
  2802	        # PARSE failure at the CLI, before it ever reaches the store — exit 2, one JSON line,
  2803	        # never a traceback.
  2804	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2805	            env=self._cli_env(Path(td)/"routing-root")
  2806	            run_id="run1_"+"a"*32
  2807	            for bad in (str(2**70), "-5"):
  2808	                result=self._cli_run(["--route-terminal",run_id,"success","--latency-ms",bad,"--json"],env)
  2809	                self.assertEqual(result.returncode,2,(bad,result.stdout,result.stderr))
  2810	                self.assertEqual(result.stdout.count("\n"),1)
  2811	                self.assertNotIn("Traceback",result.stderr)
  2812	                self.assertIn("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID",result.stdout)

  4062	    def test_adr0043_p3sec002_repair_pi_auth_signature_never_participates_in_the_real_authentication_decision(self):
  4063	        """P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): `_pi_auth_signature()`'s ONLY consumer is
  4064	        `_cache_key` (cache freshness, `catalog.py:~535`); the REAL per-pair
  4065	        authentication decision for pi is `_probe_pairs`'s `else: # pi` branch calling
  4066	        `pi_auth_provider_keys()` DIRECTLY (`catalog.py:753`), a call site
  4067	        `_pi_auth_signature` never touches. Structural proof, through the real decision
  4068	        function (`probe_inventory`'s `pairs=` fresh lane, never cached): forcing
  4069	        `_pi_auth_signature` to a FIXED non-empty value -- exactly the shape the
  4070	        pre-repair bug always produced even with an EMPTY key-set -- never changes
  4071	        whether the pi/openai-codex pair is returned; only flipping
  4072	        `pi_auth_provider_keys()` itself does. This is the guarantee the repair task
  4073	        explicitly requires: an empty `_pi_auth_signature()` can only ever cost a cache
  4074	        miss, never fabricate or suppress a decision."""
  4075	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4076	        def fake_run(argv, **kwargs):
  4077	            if argv[:2] == ("pnpm", "dlx"):
  4078	                return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="provider model\nopenai-codex gpt-5.6-luna\n", stderr="")
  4079	            return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=1, stdout="", stderr="")
  4080	        with mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, "run", side_effect=fake_run):
  4081	            # No credentials: the pair is absent, REGARDLESS of what the signature says
  4082	            # -- even a forced non-empty stub pretending to be a real signature.
  4083	            with mock.patch.object(cat, "pi_auth_provider_keys", return_value=frozenset()), \
  4084	                 mock.patch.object(cat, "_pi_auth_signature", return_value="forced-nonempty-pretending-to-be-real"):
  4085	                no_creds = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, pairs=[("pi", "openai-codex")], timeout=5.0)
  4086	            self.assertNotIn(("pi", "openai-codex"), no_creds)
  4087	            # Credentials present: the pair IS returned, regardless of the signature.
  4088	            with mock.patch.object(cat, "pi_auth_provider_keys", return_value=frozenset({"openai-codex"})), \
  4089	                 mock.patch.object(cat, "_pi_auth_signature", return_value=""):
  4090	                with_creds = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, pairs=[("pi", "openai-codex")], timeout=5.0)
  4091	            self.assertEqual(with_creds[("pi", "openai-codex")], {"gpt-5.6-luna"})
  4092	
  4093	    def test_adr0043_ac07_binary_signature_covers_opencode_codex_and_claude(self):
  4094	        """Generalizes the pre-existing opencode-only binary signature (ADR-0034 AC-08)
  4095	        to `codex`/`claude` too -- a binary swap (mtime change) must move the signature,
  4096	        and an unresolvable binary must fail closed to a fixed per-name sentinel."""
  4097	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4098	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4099	            path = Path(td) / "codex"; path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\n"); path.chmod(0o755)
  4100	            with mock.patch.object(cat.shutil, "which", side_effect=lambda name: str(path) if name == "codex" else None):
  4101	                present = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4102	                self.assertNotEqual(present, "codex:absent")
  4103	                older = os.stat(path)
  4104	                os.utime(path, (older.st_atime, older.st_mtime + 5))
  4105	                bumped = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4106	                self.assertNotEqual(present, bumped)
  4107	                self.assertEqual(cat._binary_signature("claude"), "claude:absent")

  4109	    def test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_folds_every_runtime_signature(self):
  4110	        """`_cache_key` is a SINGLE hash covering the whole probe-cache document (every
  4111	        runtime's pairs at once) -- a change in ANY one of the six new signatures must
  4112	        move the overall key, exactly like the pre-existing opencode-only binary
  4113	        signature already did."""
  4114	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4115	        base = cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4116	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_codex_auth_signature", return_value="different-codex"):
  4117	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4118	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_claude_code_auth_signature", return_value="different-claude"):
  4119	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4120	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_pi_auth_signature", return_value="different-pi"):
  4121	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4122	        # Confirms _binary_signature is actually invoked for all THREE binaries, not
  4123	        # merely that mocking it changes the result (a name-blind mock could pass even
  4124	        # if `_cache_key` only ever called it for "opencode", the pre-existing call).
  4125	        calls = []
  4126	        original = cat._binary_signature
  4127	        def spy(name):
  4128	            calls.append(name)
  4129	            return original(name)
  4130	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_binary_signature", side_effect=spy):
  4131	            cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4132	        self.assertEqual(sorted(calls), ["claude", "codex", "opencode"])
  4133	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_binary_signature", side_effect=lambda name: f"{name}:forced-different"):
  4134	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4135	
  4136	    def test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_signatures(self):
  4137	        """Trap #2 (context pack): AC-07 adds six new stat/local-read signatures to
  4138	        `_cache_key` without a single new subprocess -- the ONE subprocess the whole
  4139	        cache-key computation ever pays is `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the
  4140	        CALLER (`probe_inventory`) and passed in as `auth_signature`, never invoked from
  4141	        `_cache_key` itself (the no-argument call below never touches it)."""

  4144	            raise AssertionError("no subprocess may run while computing _cache_key")
  4145	        with mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, "run", side_effect=fail):
  4146	            cat._cache_key(self.config)  # must not raise

  4148	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_codex_refresh_keeps_the_cache_warm_and_logout_invalidates_it_via_probe_inventory(self):
  4149	        """End-to-end AC-08 property proof, through `probe_inventory` itself (never a
  4150	        real `codex logout` -- the credential file is deleted directly, same discipline
  4151	        the task instructions require): a refresh keeps the cache warm (codex is never
  4152	        reprobed); a logout misses the cache on the VERY NEXT call, still well inside
  4153	        PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) -- unlike before this package, where only opencode's
  4154	        credential change was ever visible before the TTL expired."""
  4155	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4156	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4157	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  4158	            home = Path(td) / "home"; (home / ".codex").mkdir(parents=True)
  4159	            auth = home / ".codex" / "auth.json"
  4160	            auth.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "acct_1", "access_token": "before"}}))
  4161	            cache_root = Path(td) / "root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4162	            old_path = os.environ["PATH"]; os.environ["PATH"] = f"{bins}:{old_path}"
  4163	            try:
  4164	                with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  4165	                    cold = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1000.0)
  4166	                    self.assertIn(("codex", "openai-codex"), cold)
  4167	                    auth.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "acct_1", "access_token": "AFTER"}}))
  4168	                    log.write_text("")
  4169	                    warm = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1001.0)
  4170	                    self.assertEqual(warm, cold)
  4171	                    self.assertNotIn("bin/codex", log.read_text())
  4172	                    auth.unlink()
  4173	                    log.write_text("")
  4174	                    cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1002.0)
  4175	                    self.assertIn("bin/codex", log.read_text())
  4176	            finally:
  4177	                os.environ["PATH"] = old_path

  4179	    def test_adr0043_ac09_credential_signatures_fail_closed_on_symlink_and_corrupt_or_foreign_shaped_json(self):
  4180	        """Same discipline as `test_pi_auth_provider_keys_reads_names_only_and_fails_closed`
  4181	        above, extended to the two new credential readers: a symlink is never followed,
  4182	        and corrupt/foreign-shaped JSON never raises -- always the empty signature.
  4183	
  4184	        P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3): before this repair, every case below that is a
  4185	        JSON OBJECT (never a top-level list) sailed straight past `_read_credential_json`
  4186	        (which only rejects non-dict TOP-LEVEL shapes) and reached the hashing step with
  4187	        a missing/mistyped nested field silently defaulting to a placeholder (`""`/
  4188	        `"None"`) that still got folded into a real-looking, non-empty hash -- fail-open.
  4189	        This test previously only exercised top-level LISTS (`["not", "a", "dict"]`),
  4190	        never an incomplete or mistyped OBJECT, so it stayed green while the fail-open
  4191	        gap it claimed to cover was live; the cases below close that gap: an empty
  4192	        object, a wrong-typed nested field, and a partially-populated object, for BOTH
  4193	        codex and claude-code. A fully-valid shape is asserted non-empty at the end so
  4194	        the new validation doesn't over-trigger and fail-close everything."""
  4195	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4196	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4197	            home = Path(td)
  4198	            (home / ".codex").mkdir(); (home / ".claude").mkdir()
  4199	            codex_auth = home / ".codex" / "auth.json"
  4200	            claude_creds = home / ".claude" / ".credentials.json"
  4201	            with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  4202	                self.assertEqual(cat._codex_auth_signature(), "")
  4203	                self.assertEqual(cat._claude_code_auth_signature(), "")
  4204	                real = home / "real-secret.json"
  4205	                real.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "x"}}))
  4206	                codex_auth.symlink_to(real)
  4207	                self.assertEqual(cat._codex_auth_signature(), "")
  4208	                codex_auth.unlink()
  4209	                codex_auth.write_text("not json at all")
  4210	                claude_creds.write_text("not json at all")
  4211	                self.assertEqual(cat._codex_auth_signature(), "")
  4212	                self.assertEqual(cat._claude_code_auth_signature(), "")
  4213	                codex_auth.write_text(json.dumps(["not", "a", "dict"]))
  4214	                claude_creds.write_text(json.dumps(["not", "a", "dict"]))
  4215	                self.assertEqual(cat._codex_auth_signature(), "")
# P3-repair-2 — Ronda 2 (última) — repair-agent

Paquete: 022-disponibilidad-real / P3-liveness-real. Ciclos de review consumidos: 1/2. Alcance de esta
ronda: `ai/scripts/routing_core/catalog.py` + `tests/test_routing.py`.

## Estado

COMPLETO.

## P3-F03 (critical) — el arreglo

`ai/scripts/routing_core/catalog.py`, función `pi_auth_provider_keys()` (definición en línea 348,
`_PAIR_COMMANDS` referenciado en línea ~170 arriba en el archivo, ya existente).

Antes del fix, la última línea de la función era:

```python
return frozenset(key for key in doc if isinstance(key, str))
```

Esto cumplía la mitad de lo que el docstring prometía ("provider NAMES only") — filtraba por tipo de
clave (`str`) pero NUNCA validaba (a) que el proveedor fuera uno de los auditados en `_PAIR_COMMANDS`
para el runtime `pi`, ni (b) que el valor asociado tuviera forma de objeto de credencial. Cualquier
clave string en el JSON de nivel superior — inventada o real pero con valor basura — sobrevivía.

Después del fix (línea final de la función):

```python
audited = {provider for runtime, provider in _PAIR_COMMANDS if runtime == "pi"}
return frozenset(key for key, value in doc.items()
                  if isinstance(key, str) and key in audited and isinstance(value, dict))
```

`audited` es exactamente `{"openai-codex", "anthropic"}` — los dos únicos proveedores que
`_PAIR_COMMANDS` audita para el runtime `pi` (líneas 175-176), que son también los dos únicos
proveedores que el único call site real (`_probe_pairs`, `catalog.py:803`,
`if provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys(): continue`, dentro de la rama `else: # pi`) puede llegar a
preguntar. No hay redefinición de conjunto: se reutiliza la misma tabla `_PAIR_COMMANDS` que ya es la
fuente única de verdad de pares runtime/provider auditados en todo el archivo (ver
`resolve_discovered_providers`, línea 1017, mismo patrón `{provider for _, provider in _PAIR_COMMANDS}`
para su propio caso de uso más amplio).

Por qué cierra P3-F03: el docstring de la función siempre prometió "provider NAMES only" pero la
implementación previa devolvía cualquier clave string sin más — un proveedor inventado, o un
proveedor auditado con valor no-objeto (`[]`, `null`, string, int), pasaba igual. Ahora una entrada
sólo sobrevive si es simultáneamente (a) un proveedor que `pi` audita y (b) tiene forma de objeto de
credencial — exactamente la garantía que el docstring y la firma (`_pi_auth_signature`, que hashea
este mismo keyset) siempre asumieron que existía.

## Los dos casos del hallazgo, reproducidos

### Antes del fix (usando la lógica previa, aislada en un script para no tocar el repo dos veces)

```
ANTES openai-codex_empty_list: doc={'openai-codex': []} -> keyset=frozenset({'openai-codex'}) firma_vacia=False
ANTES invented_provider_object: doc={'proveedor-inventado': {'apiKey': 'x'}} -> keyset=frozenset({'proveedor-inventado'}) firma_vacia=False
```

Coincide byte a byte con lo reproducido por el orquestador.

### Después del fix (código actual del repo, `cat.pi_auth_provider_keys()` real, HOME de fixture)

```
DESPUES openai-codex_empty_list: doc={'openai-codex': []} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
DESPUES invented_provider_object: doc={'proveedor-inventado': {'apiKey': 'x'}} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
```

Los dos casos dan ahora conjunto y firma vacíos, como exige el hallazgo.

## Cuatro formas más de romper el `auth.json` de pi (más allá de las dos del hallazgo)

Todas corridas contra el código actual, todas dan vacío:

```
DESPUES audited_provider_string_value: doc={'anthropic': 'sk-not-an-object'} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
DESPUES audited_provider_null_value: doc={'openai-codex': None} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
DESPUES audited_provider_int_value: doc={'anthropic': 12345} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
DESPUES nested_list_of_objects: doc={'openai-codex': [{'apiKey': 'x'}]} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
```

Bonus, un quinto caso — proveedor real de `_PAIR_COMMANDS` pero NO auditado para `pi` (es un proveedor
opencode), con valor bien formado (objeto):

```
DESPUES unaudited_opencode_provider_object: doc={'opencode-zen': {'apiKey': 'x'}} -> keyset=frozenset() firma_vacia=True
```

Y un caso mixto (para probar que el filtrado es por-clave, no todo-o-nada): un archivo con una entrada
válida y dos inválidas conserva SOLO la válida:

```
DESPUES mixed_valid_and_invalid: doc={'anthropic': {'apiKey': 'ok'}, 'proveedor-inventado': {'apiKey': 'x'}, 'openai-codex': []} -> keyset=frozenset({'anthropic'}) firma_vacia=False
```

## Efecto colateral: `auth.json` legítimo sin cambios

Con los dos proveedores auditados por `pi`, ambos con valor objeto (la forma real que pi escribe):

```
DESPUES legitimo_ambos_auditados: -> keyset=frozenset({'anthropic', 'openai-codex'}) (esperado frozenset({'openai-codex','anthropic'}))
```

Coincide exactamente. Además, los tests preexistentes que ejercitan el keyset legítimo y el gate real de
`_probe_pairs`/`_pi_auth_signature` (que consumen `pi_auth_provider_keys()` sin mockearla, o la mockean
para aislar el resto de la cadena) siguen en verde sin modificación — ver sección "Gates" y la mordida
más abajo. En particular:

- `test_pi_auth_provider_keys_reads_names_only_and_fails_closed` (línea 321, preexistente, sin tocar):
  usa `{"anthropic":{"apiKey":"sk-should-never-be-read"},"openai-codex":{"apiKey":"also-secret"}}` como
  caso legítimo y sigue esperando `frozenset({"anthropic","openai-codex"})` — pasa igual.
- `test_adr0043_ac09_credential_signatures_fail_closed_on_symlink_and_corrupt_or_foreign_shaped_json`
  (codex/claude-code, no toca pi) — no afectado por este cambio, sigue en verde (ver barrida abajo, no
  necesitó modificación).
- `_probe_pairs` (`catalog.py:803`) sigue llamando `pi_auth_provider_keys()` directamente, sin cambios
  de firma ni de contrato — el gate real de autenticación no se tocó, sólo se corrigió el conjunto que
  ese gate consulta.

## La barrida

Búsqueda hecha fuera de `catalog.py` (sólo para descartar otro lector de credenciales de terceros, sin
tocar nada fuera de alcance): `grep -rn "Path.home()\|\.pi/agent\|\.codex/auth\|\.claude/\.credentials\|auth\.json\|credentials\.json" ai/scripts --include="*.py"`.
Único hallazgo relevante: `ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py:1080`, función `auth_state()`, rama `claude` —
```python
credentials = Path.home() / ".claude/.credentials.json"
return "ok" if credentials.exists() and credentials.stat().st_size > 0 else "needed"
```
Esto NUNCA abre ni parsea el archivo como JSON (sólo `.exists()`/`.stat().st_size`), así que no hay
forma/tipo que validar — no es una guarda floja (no promete validar forma que luego no valida), es un
heurístico de presencia para el status humano de `set-agents --status`, ya documentado como tal en el
comentario adyacente ("no stable status command; same heuristic install.sh uses"). Fuera de alcance
(no es `catalog.py`) y no amerita anotarse como hallazgo aparte porque no hay brecha entre lo que dice
hacer y lo que hace.

   353	    def test_pi_auth_provider_keys_rejects_unaudited_and_non_object_entries(self):
   354	        """P3-F03 repair (022 PKG-3 repair round 2): the docstring above has always
   355	        promised "provider NAMES only", but before this repair the implementation kept
   356	        EVERY top-level string key verbatim, regardless of whether `_PAIR_COMMANDS`
   357	        actually audits it for the `pi` runtime or whether its value even looked like a
   358	        credential entry. `test_pi_auth_provider_keys_reads_names_only_and_fails_closed`
   359	        above only ever exercised a top-level LIST (`["not","a","dict"]`) for the
   360	        "foreign shape" case, which `isinstance(doc, dict)` already rejects before the
   361	        per-key logic even runs -- it never caught an invalid OBJECT entry. Reproduced
   362	        live by the orchestrator with a fixture HOME: `{"openai-codex": []}` and
   363	        `{"proveedor-inventado": {...}}` both produced a NON-empty keyset/signature
   364	        before this fix. Every case below must yield the empty set."""
   365	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   366	            home=Path(td); agent=home/".pi/agent"; agent.mkdir(parents=True)
   367	            cases = [
   368	                {"openai-codex": []},                       # audited key, non-object value (list)
   369	                {"proveedor-inventado": {"apiKey": "x"}},    # unaudited key, otherwise well-shaped
   370	                {"anthropic": "sk-not-an-object"},           # audited key, non-object value (string)
   371	                {"openai-codex": None},                      # audited key, non-object value (null)
   372	                {"anthropic": 12345},                        # audited key, non-object value (int)
   373	                {"opencode-zen": {"apiKey": "x"}},           # a REAL _PAIR_COMMANDS provider, but not for pi
   374	            ]
   375	            with mock.patch.object(routing_catalog.Path,"home",return_value=home):
   376	                for doc in cases:
   377	                    (agent/"auth.json").write_text(json.dumps(doc))
   378	                    self.assertEqual(routing_catalog.pi_auth_provider_keys(),frozenset(),
   379	                                     msg=f"expected empty keyset for {doc!r}")
   380	                # Mixed: only the audited key with an object value survives -- invalid
   381	                # entries are dropped individually, never all-or-nothing across the file.
   382	                (agent/"auth.json").write_text(json.dumps(
   383	                    {"anthropic": {"apiKey": "ok"}, "proveedor-inventado": {"apiKey": "x"}, "openai-codex": []}))
   384	                self.assertEqual(routing_catalog.pi_auth_provider_keys(),frozenset({"anthropic"}))
   385	                # Control: a fully legitimate file (both audited providers, both objects)
   386	                # is completely unaffected by the filter -- same result as before the fix.
   387	                (agent/"auth.json").write_text(json.dumps(
   388	                    {"openai-codex": {"apiKey": "sk-1"}, "anthropic": {"apiKey": "sk-2"}}))
   389	                self.assertEqual(routing_catalog.pi_auth_provider_keys(),
   390	                                 frozenset({"openai-codex","anthropic"}))

  3975	        """AC-07 (stat-only, no subprocess) + AC-08's two properties for codex: a refresh

  3985	                "tokens": {"access_token": "secret-before", "account_id": "acct_123",

  4041	    def test_adr0043_ac07_pi_auth_signature_folds_provider_keys_and_the_pinned_version(self):
  4042	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4043	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "pi_auth_provider_keys", return_value=frozenset({"anthropic"})):
  4044	            with_anthropic = cat._pi_auth_signature()
  4045	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "pi_auth_provider_keys", return_value=frozenset()):
  4046	            without = cat._pi_auth_signature()

  4049	        # foreign-owned, corrupt/foreign-shaped JSON) must fail closed to the EMPTY
  4050	        # STRING specifically -- not merely "different from a real signature". Before
  4051	        # this repair, `without` above was a fixed non-empty hash of

  4053	        # merely "different" rather than the empty sentinel `_cache_key`'s other five
  4054	        # signatures already use to mean "skip the cache, this runtime is unreadable".
  4055	        self.assertEqual(without, "")
  4056	        self.assertTrue(with_anthropic)
  4057	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "pi_auth_provider_keys", return_value=frozenset({"anthropic"})), \
  4058	             mock.patch.object(cat, "PI_PINNED_VERSION", "9.9.9"):

  4063	        """P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): `_pi_auth_signature()`'s ONLY consumer is
  4064	        `_cache_key` (cache freshness, `catalog.py:~535`); the REAL per-pair
  4065	        authentication decision for pi is `_probe_pairs`'s `else: # pi` branch calling

  4069	        `_pi_auth_signature` to a FIXED non-empty value -- exactly the shape the

  4072	        `pi_auth_provider_keys()` itself does. This is the guarantee the repair task
  4073	        explicitly requires: an empty `_pi_auth_signature()` can only ever cost a cache
  4074	        miss, never fabricate or suppress a decision."""

  4093	    def test_adr0043_ac07_binary_signature_covers_opencode_codex_and_claude(self):
  4094	        """Generalizes the pre-existing opencode-only binary signature (ADR-0034 AC-08)
  4095	        to `codex`/`claude` too -- a binary swap (mtime change) must move the signature,
  4096	        and an unresolvable binary must fail closed to a fixed per-name sentinel."""
  4097	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4098	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4099	            path = Path(td) / "codex"; path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\n"); path.chmod(0o755)
  4100	            with mock.patch.object(cat.shutil, "which", side_effect=lambda name: str(path) if name == "codex" else None):
  4101	                present = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4102	                self.assertNotEqual(present, "codex:absent")
  4103	                older = os.stat(path)
  4104	                os.utime(path, (older.st_atime, older.st_mtime + 5))
  4105	                bumped = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4106	                self.assertNotEqual(present, bumped)
  4107	                self.assertEqual(cat._binary_signature("claude"), "claude:absent")

+def resolve_ceiling(config: dict, provider: str) -> tuple[str, set[str] | None]:
+    """ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2): the tri-state ceiling every one of this file's three
+    consumers (`_probe_pairs`, `_read_probe_cache`, `build_snapshot`) reads through now,
+    replacing the two-state `_configured_models` this function inlined and removed. That
+    function only ever returned a bare `set()`, which collapsed two genuinely different
+    TOML shapes into the identical value: "no `[catalog]` key at all for this provider"
+    and "curated as an explicit empty allowlist". The FIRST must mean "no ceiling, the
+    live probe result is the routable set"; the SECOND must mean "never route this
+    provider, no matter what the probe shows" -- conflating them is exactly why
+    `_probe_pairs`'s `if not allowed: continue` skipped a provider ENTIRELY the moment its
+    `[catalog]` key was absent, which in turn forced editing `models.toml` to add any new
+    provider (the defect this whole package exists to remove).
+
+    Three states, a pure reflection of the literal TOML shape under `[catalog].<key>`:
+      - key absent from `[catalog]` entirely (or `provider` has no dedicated key at all,
+        e.g. a future `PROVIDERS` entry added without one) => `("auto", None)` -- no
+        ceiling; whatever the live probe reports for this provider IS the routable set,
+        unfiltered by any curated allowlist.
+      - key present as `[]` => `("veto", set())` -- curated, explicit "never route this
+        provider", regardless of what the live probe would otherwise show.
+      - key present as a non-empty list => `("curated", set-of-ids)` -- the audited
+        ceiling every model must be a member of (today's only behavior, unchanged).
+
+    Same five-site lockstep `_configured_models` used to document, site (2) of 5: (1)
+    `models.toml`'s `[catalog]` table itself; (2) this key map (`_CATALOG_KEYS` above); (3)
+    `build_snapshot`'s own `configured_models` comprehension (below); (4)
+    `models_config.load_config`'s optional-key validation loop for the same two TOML keys
+    (accepts `[]` now, AC-04); (5) `models_config.emit`'s preservation loop for the same
+    two keys (round-trips absence AND `[]`, never inventing either).
+
+    Per ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2, decision recorded by Federico 2026-08-12, spec AC-04): in
+    PRACTICE only `opencode-zen`/`opencode-go` ever reach the "auto"/"veto" branches --
+    `models_config.load_config` dies if `[catalog].claude`/`.codex` are absent or empty, so
+    `openai-codex`/`anthropic` are structurally guaranteed "curated" by the time any caller
+    here has a validated config. `resolve_ceiling` itself stays provider-agnostic and
+    enforces nothing about WHICH providers may reach which state -- that restriction lives
+    at the validation layer (`models_config.py`) on purpose, same split ADR-0042 already
+    documents for the rest of this registry (billing-kind logic never bleeding into
+    provider identity; here, "which providers get tri-state" never bleeding into "what a
+    given TOML shape means").
+    """
+    key = _CATALOG_KEYS.get(provider)
+    if key is None or key not in config.get("catalog", {}):
+        return ("auto", None)
+    models = config["catalog"][key]
     if not isinstance(models, (list, tuple)):
-        return set()
-    return {item for item in models if isinstance(item, str) and item}
+        return ("auto", None)  # defensive only -- load_config already dies on this shape
+    cleaned = {item for item in models if isinstance(item, str) and item}
+    if not cleaned:
+        return ("veto", set())
+    return ("curated", cleaned)

+# ------------------------------------------------------------------ ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3)
+# Per-runtime credential signatures, AC-07: closes "de-auth invisible for up to
+# PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) in 3 of 4 runtimes" -- `_cache_key` used to fold in ONLY
+# opencode's live subprocess-based auth signature (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`
+# above). Every signature below is `stat`/local-read only -- ZERO new subprocesses
+# (the one subprocess this whole cache-key computation ever pays stays
+# `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the CALLER and passed in, never invoked
+# from here -- see `test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_
+# signatures` in tests/test_routing.py and evidence/P3-implementer.md's proof). Same
+# security discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` throughout (AC-09): `lstat` (never a
+# symlink), a regular file, this uid, ONLY named fields ever read -- never a whole-file
+# hash or mtime, because `~/.claude/.credentials.json` also holds an unrelated
+# `mcpOAuth` block (today a Vercel token, live-measured) that rotates on ITS OWN MCP
+# refresh cycle with zero relation to the Claude Code provider credential; hashing the
+# file or its mtime would rotate this signature on every MCP token refresh, defeating
+# the cache for a reason that has nothing to do with what this signature exists to
+# track. Every value is hashed before being folded into `_cache_key` -- never logged,
+# never placed in an envelope, never a raw credential value.
+
+
+def _read_credential_json(path: Path) -> dict | None:
+    """Shared fail-closed read for a local per-runtime credential file: `lstat` (never
+    a symlink), a regular file, owned by this uid, valid JSON, a dict -- ANY surprise is
+    `None`, never raised, same discipline as `pi_auth_provider_keys` above. The caller
+    reads only specific NAMED fields from the result; this function itself never
+    inspects field values, so widening what a signature reads is always a one-line,
+    auditable change at the call site, never here."""
+    try:
+        st = path.lstat()
+        if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
+            return None
+        doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError):
+        return None
+    return doc if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
+
+
+def _codex_auth_signature() -> str:
+    """AC-07/AC-09: codex's per-runtime signature -- `~/.codex/auth.json`, ONLY
+    `tokens.account_id` (real account identity; codex is the one runtime of the three
+    whose credential file carries one, ADR-0043) and whether `OPENAI_API_KEY` is
+    present (a boolean, never the key's own value). NEVER `tokens.access_token`/
+    `tokens.refresh_token`/`tokens.id_token`/`last_refresh` -- token material and its
+    timestamp, which rotate on every refresh (AC-08 property 1: a signature built from
+    a rotating field would miss the cache on every routine OAuth renewal). File absent,
+    foreign-owned, a symlink, or unreadable -- fail-closed to the empty string, which
+    never equals a real signature, so the cache is simply skipped (a fresh probe runs),
+    never fabricated as a match (AC-08 property 2: this is what makes a real logout/
+    delete visible immediately).
+
+    P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): a VALID JSON object that is missing `tokens`
+    or whose `tokens.account_id` isn't a string (corrupt file, foreign shape, a `{}`
+    written mid-write) must fail closed too, exactly like a missing/foreign-owned/
+    unreadable file already does via `_read_credential_json` -- shape and type of every
+    field this signature actually reads are validated BEFORE hashing, never after. Before
+    this fix, a missing/mistyped `tokens.account_id` silently defaulted to `""` and STILL
+    got folded into a hash (`account_id=|api_key_present=...`), producing a fixed
+    non-empty signature instead of the required empty one -- fail-open, because that
+    non-empty value looked exactly like a real (if account-id-less) signature to
+    `_cache_key`, so a corrupt-shaped file could still warm/serve a cache entry."""
+    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".codex/auth.json")
+    if doc is None:
+        return ""
+    tokens = doc.get("tokens")
+    if not isinstance(tokens, dict):
+        return ""
+    account_id = tokens.get("account_id")
+    if not isinstance(account_id, str):
+        return ""
+    has_api_key = isinstance(doc.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"), str)
+    material = f"account_id={account_id}|api_key_present={has_api_key}"
+    return hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
+
+
+def _claude_code_auth_signature() -> str:
+    """AC-07/AC-09: claude-code's per-runtime signature -- `~/.claude/.credentials.json`,
+    ONLY the three `claudeAiOauth` fields measured NON-rotating across a refresh
+    (`scopes`, `subscriptionType`, `rateLimitTier` -- attributes of the OAuth grant/plan
+    itself, not of the token instance; evidence/P3-implementer.md). NEVER
+    `accessToken`/`refreshToken`/`expiresAt`/`refreshTokenExpiresAt` (token material,
+    AC-09: "hashear el refreshToken no se hace"), and NEVER the file's OTHER top-level
+    key `mcpOAuth` (the trap this module's own section docstring names).
+    ADR-0043's declared limit: `claudeAiOauth` carries NO account-identity field (unlike
+    codex's `tokens.account_id`) -- this signature therefore detects PRESENCE/ABSENCE
+    (logout, deletion, file absent) but never a same-plan account swap without an
+    intervening logout. Closing that gap would mean hashing `refreshToken`, which this
+    package explicitly does not do.
+
+    P3-SEC-001 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): `claudeAiOauth` being present as a dict is not
+    enough -- shape and type of the three fields this signature actually reads are
+    validated BEFORE hashing (`scopes` a list, `subscriptionType`/`rateLimitTier` a
+    string each); any absence or wrong type fails closed to the empty string. Before this
+    fix, `{"claudeAiOauth": {}}` (or any object missing one of the three fields) still
+    produced a fixed non-empty hash of the literal string `"None"` in place of each
+    missing field, indistinguishable from a real signature to `_cache_key` -- fail-open
+    on a corrupt/foreign-shaped file."""
+    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".claude/.credentials.json")
+    if doc is None:
+        return ""
+    oauth = doc.get("claudeAiOauth")
+    if not isinstance(oauth, dict):
+        return ""
+    scopes, subscription_type, rate_limit_tier = (oauth.get(f) for f in
+                                                   ("scopes", "subscriptionType", "rateLimitTier"))
+    if not isinstance(scopes, list) or not isinstance(subscription_type, str) \
+            or not isinstance(rate_limit_tier, str):
+        return ""
+    parts = [str(scopes), subscription_type, rate_limit_tier]
+    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(parts).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
+
+
+def _pi_auth_signature() -> str:
+    """AC-07: pi's per-runtime signature folds `pi_auth_provider_keys()` (already
+    stat/local-read only, zero subprocess, spike Q2 discipline -- untouched by this
+    package beyond the uid check, P3-SEC-002) with `PI_PINNED_VERSION` (the EXACT pi
+    release the probe invokes, above) -- a version bump is itself credential-relevant: a
+    different pinned pi release could read/write a differently-shaped auth.json. Provider
+    NAMES only (never a token value), hashed before folding into `_cache_key`.
+
+    P3-SEC-002 repair (022 PKG-3, AC-09): an EMPTY key-set (file absent, symlink,
+    foreign-owned, corrupt/foreign-shaped JSON -- anything `pi_auth_provider_keys()`
+    itself already fails closed on) must fail closed HERE too, to the empty string, same
+    as `_codex_auth_signature`/`_claude_code_auth_signature` above. Before this fix, an
+    empty key-set was folded into a hash alongside `PI_PINNED_VERSION` regardless,
+    producing a fixed non-empty signature for "pi has no credentials" -- fail-OPEN,
+    because a non-empty signature is indistinguishable, at the `_cache_key` level, from a
+    real credential state. This is safe to change: `_pi_auth_signature`'s only consumer
+    is `_cache_key` (cache freshness), never the real per-pair authentication gate
+    (`_probe_pairs` calls `pi_auth_provider_keys()` directly, catalog.py:753) -- an empty
+    signature can only ever cost a cache miss (a fresh, correctly-gated probe runs next),
+    never fabricate or suppress which pairs are actually authenticated."""
+    keys = pi_auth_provider_keys()
+    if not keys:
+        return ""
+    names = ",".join(sorted(keys))
+    return hashlib.sha256(f"{names}|{PI_PINNED_VERSION}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

+def prune_legacy_probe_cache(legacy_root) -> bool:
+    """AC-10 (022 PKG-3): a single probe cache lives at the store's cache root now
+    (`RoutingStore.ensure_cache_root()`, `~/.local/state/set-agentes/routing-v2`) --
+    this removes the STALE SIBLING file at the old, divergent location
+    (`STATE_DIR/probe-cache.json`, what `--route-doctor`/`--doctor-all`/the 'Estado
+    general' panel used to read while the decision path already read/wrote the
+    routing-v2 one -- two caches confirmed diverging live, ADR-0043).
+
+    Same security discipline as `_write_probe_cache`, mirrored exactly: the directory
+    must pass `_validate_cache_dir` (0700, this uid, no symlink) and the file itself
+    must independently be a regular, this-uid file (never following a symlink) before
+    it is ever unlinked -- any surprise leaves it untouched (fail-closed: an
+    unrecognized foreign object is never deleted). `STATE_DIR` also holds
+    `config.toml`/`model-preference.toml` and other real state -- this function NEVER
+    touches the directory itself or any other file in it, only this one legacy
+    `probe-cache.json`. Returns True only when a file was actually removed (for the
+    caller's own best-effort bookkeeping); False for "nothing to prune" and every
+    fail-closed branch alike -- the caller never needs to distinguish them, pruning is
+    always best-effort."""
+    root = Path(legacy_root)
+    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
+        return False
+    path = root / "probe-cache.json"
+    try:
+        st = path.lstat()
+    except OSError:
+        return False
+    if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
+        return False
+    try:
+        path.unlink()
+    except OSError:
+        return False
+    return True
+

+# AC-05 layer 4 (022 PKG-2): deterministic upper bound on how many NEWLY-synthesized
+# routes a single discovered provider ever contributes to one snapshot, regardless of
+# ceiling state (curated OR auto). Live-measured 2026-08-12: opencode-zen alone lists
+# 58-61 models (`opencode models opencode --pure`), already comfortably under this cap
+# today (curated at 60) -- the cap exists for what today's curated allowlist happens to
+# bound "by accident": an "auto" provider (no `[catalog]` key at all) has NO curated
+# ceiling to keep the raw live-probe count in check, so a provider whose upstream
+# catalog grows past this constant in the future (or is auto from day one) still gets a
+# hard, predictable stop, not silent unbounded growth of the synthesized-route pool that
+# feeds `service.route`'s candidate sort. Alphabetical truncation (`sorted()`, never
+# probe/dict iteration order) keeps which models survive the cut IDENTICAL across runs
+# for the same inputs -- "deterministic" is about repeatability, not about the cut being
+# the "best" N by any other measure. Applied to the post-filter (curated/excluded ids
+# already dropped) candidate list per provider, so the cap bounds actual pool growth,
+# not merely how many raw ids were considered.
+_DISCOVERED_ROUTE_CAP_PER_PROVIDER = 80

+        the "no dedicated catalog key at all" edge that also resolves to auto."""
+        from routing_core import catalog as cat
+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "opencode-zen"),
+                         ("curated", set(self.config["catalog"]["opencode_zen"])))
+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "openai-codex"),
+                         ("curated", set(self.config["catalog"]["codex"])))
+        auto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); del auto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]
+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(auto_cfg, "opencode-zen"), ("auto", None))
+        veto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); veto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_go"] = []
+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(veto_cfg, "opencode-go"), ("veto", set()))
+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "totally-unregistered-provider"), ("auto", None))
+
+    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac04_probe_pairs_probes_a_provider_with_no_catalog_key_instead_of_skipping_it(self):
+        """The defect this package exists to fix (context pack, verbatim): before this
+        package, `_probe_pairs`'s `if not allowed: continue` skipped ANY provider absent
+        from `[catalog]` entirely -- exactly what forced editing models.toml to add a
+        provider. `auto_cfg` removes `[catalog].opencode_zen`; the SAME CLI stub output
+        is probed once against `auto_cfg` (must surface, unfiltered) and once against
+        the real curated `self.config` (must still be filtered out -- the model is not
+        in the curated allowlist), proving the tri-state distinction end to end. Also
+        covers AC-05 layer 1: a pair absent from `_PAIR_COMMANDS` is never probed no
+        matter what ceiling state a (bogus) provider string would resolve to."""
+        from routing_core import catalog as cat
+        auto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); del auto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]
+
+        def fake_run(argv, **kwargs):
+            if argv[1:4] == ("auth", "list", "--pure"):
+                return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="●  OpenCode Zen api\n", stderr="")
+            return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="opencode/a-brand-new-zen-model-not-curated-anywhere\n", stderr="")
+        with mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, "run", side_effect=fake_run):
+            auto_result = cat.probe_inventory(auto_cfg, cache_root=None,
+                                              pairs=[("opencode", "opencode-zen"), ("opencode", "not-an-audited-provider")])
+            curated_result = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=None, pairs=[("opencode", "opencode-zen")])
+        self.assertEqual(auto_result.get(("opencode", "opencode-zen")), {"a-brand-new-zen-model-not-curated-anywhere"})
+        self.assertNotIn(("opencode", "not-an-audited-provider"), auto_result)  # AC-05 layer 1
+        self.assertNotIn(("opencode", "opencode-zen"), curated_result)  # same stdout, curated ceiling still filters it
+
+    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac04_read_probe_cache_auto_mode_never_goes_permanently_empty(self):
+        """The single easiest regression to reintroduce here (context pack, verbatim): a
+        naive port of the pre-tri-state one-liner (`set(models) & <curated ceiling>`)
+        intersects an "auto" provider's cached models against an EMPTY ceiling (there is
+        none), emptying the cache entry on every single read -- the caller then re-probes
+        on every decision, up to PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS (60s) for pi. Writes a
+        cache document directly (bypassing any probe) for an auto-ceiling provider and
+        asserts the read survives with its models intact, never silently emptied."""
+        from routing_core import catalog as cat
+        auto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); del auto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+            cache_root = Path(td) / "root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
+            key = cat._cache_key(auto_cfg)
+            doc = {"key": key, "at": 1000.0, "pairs": {"opencode|opencode-zen": ["some-live-only-zen-model"]}}
+            path = cache_root / "probe-cache.json"; path.write_text(json.dumps(doc)); path.chmod(0o600)
+            out = cat._read_probe_cache(cache_root, key, 1000.5, auto_cfg)
+        self.assertEqual(out, {("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"some-live-only-zen-model"}})
+
+    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac06_curated_row_against_auto_or_veto_ceiling_raises_the_named_error(self):
+        """AC-06: differentiated from the generic CATALOG_INVALID every other
+        build_snapshot shape failure raises -- same precedent as
+        CATALOG_FAMILY_COLLISION. Exercised through a synthetic td-based catalog file
+        (never routes.v1.toml itself, which stays untouched -- non-goal)."""
+        from routing_core import catalog as cat
+        model = sorted(self.config["catalog"]["opencode_zen"])[0]
+        full_roster = sorted({row["role"] for row in self.roster})
+        row = dict(provider="opencode-zen", model=model, family="zen-test", effort="medium",
+                  tier="balanced", roles=full_roster, tools=["read"], curated_priority=1)
+        for state, mutate in (("auto", lambda cfg: cfg["catalog"].pop("opencode_zen")),
+                              ("veto", lambda cfg: cfg["catalog"].__setitem__("opencode_zen", []))):
+            cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); mutate(cfg)
+            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+                path = self._write_catalog(td, [row])
+                with self.assertRaisesRegex(routing.RoutingError, "CATALOG_CEILING_REQUIRED", msg=state):
+                    routing_catalog.build_snapshot(path, self.roster, cfg)
+        # Contrast: today's real config resolves this SAME provider as "curated" -- the
+        # new check is additive, never a wider net that swallows the curated case too.
+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(self.config, "opencode-zen")[0], "curated")
+
+    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac05_layer2_auto_ceiling_reaches_only_the_synthesized_path(self):
+        """AC-05 layer 2: an "auto" provider can never enter the CURATED snapshot (the
+        negative half, reusing AC-06's own error) -- this proves the POSITIVE half in the
+        same test: the identical auto-ceiling config, through build_effective_snapshot
+        with a live inventory, reaches routing ONLY via the synthesized path, its
+        route_id present in the returned `inferred` set (the MODEL_METADATA_INFERRED
+        marker `service.py` reads)."""
+        from routing_core import catalog as cat
+        auto_cfg = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config)); del auto_cfg["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]
+        auto_cfg["routing"] = dict(auto_cfg["routing"], discovered_providers=["opencode-zen"])
+        model = "a-live-only-auto-model"
+        inventory = {("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {model}}
+        catalog_path = ROOT / "ai/catalogs/routes.v1.toml"
+        snapshot, inferred = routing_catalog.build_effective_snapshot(catalog_path, self.roster, auto_cfg, inventory)
+        synthesized = [r for r in snapshot.routes if r.provider == "opencode-zen" and r.model == model]
+        self.assertEqual(len(synthesized), 1)
+        self.assertIn(synthesized[0].route_id, inferred)
+        full_roster = sorted({row["role"] for row in self.roster})
+        curated_row = dict(provider="opencode-zen", model=model, family="zen-test", effort="medium",
+                           tier="balanced", roles=full_roster, tools=["read"], curated_priority=1)
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+            path = self._write_catalog(td, [curated_row])
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(routing.RoutingError, "CATALOG_CEILING_REQUIRED"):
+                routing_catalog.build_snapshot(path, self.roster, auto_cfg)
+
+    def test_adr0042_pkg2_ac05_layer3_billing_rank_still_fails_closed_expensive_under_auto(self):
+        """AC-05 layer 3: `billing_rank` is keyed purely by provider string
+        (PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND), never by config or ceiling state -- switching a
+        provider's [catalog] ceiling to auto/veto cannot change its billing
+        classification. Named as its own layer in AC-05 and re-verified here directly,
+        not merely assumed from PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND's own pre-existing test."""
+        from routing_core import catalog as cat

+        self.assertEqual(cat.billing_rank("opencode-go", "any-model"), 0)  # subscription, cheap
+        self.assertEqual(cat.billing_rank("totally-unaudited-provider", "any-model"), 1)  # fail-closed

+        self.assertEqual(cat.resolve_ceiling(auto_cfg, "opencode-zen")[0], "auto")
+        self.assertEqual(cat.billing_rank("opencode-zen", "any-model"), 1)  # unchanged by ceiling state
